# KDIGO 2021 Glomerular Diseases Guideline — References

**Source:** Kidney Disease: Improving Global Outcomes (KDIGO) Glomerular Diseases Work Group. *KDIGO 2021 Clinical Practice Guideline for the Management of Glomerular Diseases.* Kidney Int. 2021;100(4S):S1–S276. DOI: 10.1016/j.kint.2021.05.021.

**Main guideline PDF SHA-256:** `8ed871ec098c7eba1cfb0ff6bf2355a6c422166b2691aac138f0273307b2acff`  
**Data supplement PDF SHA-256:** `2c694e198964bc790f07489ec2f6bc3cbb0c4968f71902c4e4e06953a8931d76`

> Version note: the supplied main PDF is the 2021 guideline with May-2024 warning stamps stating that Chapter 9 (AAV) and Chapter 10 (lupus nephritis) are outdated and directing readers to external 2024 updates. The replacement chapters are not contained in the supplied files.
